  {
    "subsystem": "did-the-work-fully-address-the-issue-hints-user-notes-and-approved-plan-requirements",
    "total_occurrences": 7,
    "plan_count": 6,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-013",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the package is internally inconsistent. the plan body says to keep `activepairdata` in `usestate` and make phase 2 optional, but the attached metadata and success criteria still describe the previous derive-via-`usememo` / remove-`setactivepairdata` / simplify-`onpairclick` plan. that means the approved-plan requirements are only partially coherent as presented.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "fix-the-denormalized-20260331-0225"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-108",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: the linux distro note is documentation-only; the generated install command in step 7 still runs `apt-get install python3.10-venv python3.10-dev ffmpeg` without any pre-check that the package exists. users on ubuntu 24.04+ who ignore the doc and try to copy-paste the command will hit a confusing `e: unable to locate package python3.10-venv` from apt rather than a targeted error from commandutils.ts. a one-line detection pre-check (e.g., `apt-cache show python3.10-venv >/dev/null 2>&1 || { echo 'install deadsnakes ppa first \u2014 see readme'; exit 1; }`) would convert the silent failure into an actionable error, but the plan does not add this.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-145",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: checked the revised v4 body against `docs/wan2gp_fork_migration_plan.md` sprint 2. the repo plan doc still lists an \"ltx-2 `self_refiner` smoke against the pre-sprint behavioral baseline\" as a sprint 2 verification gate and repeats that smoke in the functionality-preservation checks, but the revised execution steps no longer schedule that smoke anywhere; it survives only as an info-level metadata criterion. because `wan2gp/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` remains an explicit sprint 2 deliverable, the revised plan still only partially carries forward the verification package described in the source migration plan.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-153",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: worker integration: the migration doc describes the per-call profile override gap as involving reigh-worker adapter handling of override_profile before constructing vibecomfy sessionconfig, but the plan limits sprint 1 per-run work to the vibecomfy cli/runtime. that may be acceptable for a vibecomfy-library mvp, but it does not fully exercise the production-facing per-task override semantics called out in the overall context.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "sprint-1-vibecomfy-memory-20260506-0147"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-155",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: a residual issue-hint tension remains for resolution handling. the plan acknowledges current `vibecomfy run` lacks a resolution flag, but allows the adapter to leave resolution and other template-specific values unwired for sprint 2. because `z_image_turbo` and `qwen_image_2512` are marked vibecomfy-supported direct routes, this may not preserve the existing worker task-parameter contract for tasks that provide a non-default `resolution`.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-173",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: model warming is explicitly deferred to v2 (assumption #6). the idea text demands the new path 'reach workflow execution materially faster than the cold fresh path'. v1 meets this for deps (67 min \u2192 ~10 min) but a first-encounter workflow still incurs a 10-50gb hf download. the success criterion for run b model reuse depends on run a having performed that download. plan accepts this tradeoff openly via open question #1 \u2014 flagging because the idea's 'reusable validation environment' framing could be read as covering models too.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "build-a-reusable-prebuilt-20260512-2351"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-174",
        "concern": "did the work fully address the issue hints, user notes, and approved plan requirements?: node-schema validation (issue_hints-3) is wired via a `vibecomfy.cli nodes verify` probe (step 2.4d, step 8.3), but assumption #9 admits the subcommand 'either already exists or is a trivial read-only addition'. the plan does not enumerate adding it to the vibecomfy repo as a planned change. if it does not exist, the executor either silently no-ops the probe (false success) or adds a vibecomfy patch that is out of the stated touchpoints. open question #3 explicitly defers verifying its existence.",

  {
    "subsystem": "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them",
    "total_occurrences": 6,
    "plan_count": 4,
    "entries": [
      {
        "id": "DEBT-109",
        "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the step 2 retry loop uses a naked `sleep 2` between attempts, which adds up to 6 seconds of latency on the happy path when the first patch eventually succeeds on attempt 2 or 3. for pods where supabase is reachable but slow during the initial moments of a cold runpod start, the retry logic is fine. however, if the first attempt fails with a tls handshake delay and the retry loop sleeps 2s per attempt regardless of whether curl itself has been blocking for tens of seconds, the total startup latency penalty could be substantial. this is a minor tuning concern, not a correctness issue.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "migrate-the-python-worker-20260409-0353"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-146",
        "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: finding callers of the changed module shows that `shared.utils.self_refiner` is consumed by `wan2gp/wgp.py`, `wan2gp/models/ltx2/ltx2_handler.py`, multiple ltx2 pipeline modules, and `wan2gp/models/wan/any2video.py`. the revised validation steps still only drive the three bridge getters in `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py`; none of the scheduled commands execute a real `self_refiner` caller, so the plan does not yet verify the main caller shapes for the other explicit sprint 2 file change.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "execute-sprint-2-of-the-20260421-2202"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-159",
        "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked direct queue caller values in `db_task_to_generation_task()`: direct image tasks may carry `resolution`, `steps`/`num_inference_steps`, `seed`, and `override_profile`. the revised adapter plan covers seed, steps, and memory profile but not guaranteed resolution propagation, so it does not handle all relevant direct-route caller arguments for a supported vibecomfy direct route.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "sprint-2-adapter-seam-and-20260506-0249"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-185",
        "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: step 5.2 `register_worker_record(..., variant_label: str)` reads `args.backend`, `args.worker_profile`, `args.selector_namespace`, `args.selector_version`, `args.worker_contract_version`. step 9.3 says the prebuilt variant inherits the shared parser. variant_fresh's `_finalize_args` at main.py:115-132 also performs variant-specific normalization (e.g. when `args.backend == 'vibecomfy' && args.selector_namespace == 'production'`, switches to `_live_test_selector_namespace()`). the plan does not enumerate whether `_finalize_args` runs for `--variant prebuilt` too. if it doesn't, prebuilt runs against production selector namespace by default \u2014 different behavior than fresh.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "build-a-reusable-prebuilt-20260512-2351"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-186",
        "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: callers of `_uv_sync_shell` (new in step 3.1): only `run_install` wraps it (`extras=('cuda124',)`, `with_locked=false`). variant_update.py:69-79 has a separate `remote_uv_sync` that uses `--locked`. plan does not migrate variant_update's remote_uv_sync to call `_uv_sync_shell(..., with_locked=true)` \u2014 variant_update stays untouched per assumption #4. so the new `with_locked` parameter has no caller. minor but adds unused api surface.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "build-a-reusable-prebuilt-20260512-2351"
        ]
      },

        Debt guidance:
        - These are known accepted limitations. Do not re-flag them unless the current plan makes them worse, broadens them, or fails to contain them.
        - Prefix every new concern with a subsystem tag followed by a colon, for example `Timeout recovery: retry backoff remains brittle`.
        - When a concern is recurring debt that still needs to be flagged, prefix it with `Recurring debt:` after the subsystem tag, for example `Timeout recovery: Recurring debt: retry backoff remains brittle`.

Read this file first — it contains 5 checks, each with a question and guidance.
For each check, investigate the codebase, then add your findings to the `findings` array for that check.

Each finding needs:
- "detail": what you specifically checked and what you found (at least a full sentence)
- "flagged": true if this describes a difference, risk, or tension — even if you think it's justified. false only if purely informational with no possible downside.
- Every check must end with at least one finding. Never leave a `findings` array empty. If you found no issue, add one detailed `flagged: false` finding explaining what you checked and why it appears clear.

When in doubt, flag it — the gate can accept tradeoffs, but it can't act on findings it never sees.

Good: {"detail": "Checked callers of nthroot_mod in solveset.py line 1205 — passes prime moduli only, consistent with the fix.", "flagged": false}
Good: {"detail": "The fix handles empty tuples but not single-element tuples which need a trailing comma.", "flagged": true}
Bad: {"detail": "No issue found", "flagged": false}  ← too brief, will be rejected
Bad: {"detail": "The hints suggest approach X but the plan uses Y. However Y is consistent with X's intent.", "flagged": false}  ← a different approach than the hints IS a flag. You found a divergence — flag it. The gate decides if it's acceptable.

After filling in checks, add any additional concerns to the `flags` array (e.g., security, performance, dependencies).
Use the standard format (id, concern, category, severity_hint, evidence). This array can be empty.

Workflow: read the file → investigate → read file again → add finding → write file back. Repeat for each check.

        Additional guidelines:
        - Robustness level: standard. Use balanced judgment. Flag significant risks, but do not spend flags on minor polish or executor-obvious boilerplate.
        - Over-engineering: prefer the simplest approach that fully solves the problem.
        - Reuse existing flag IDs when the same concern is still open.
        - `verified_flag_ids`: list flag IDs from prior iterations that the revised plan actually resolves (e.g., if the plan was revised to fix FLAG-001, and you confirm the fix is correct, include "FLAG-001"). Only include flags you've verified — don't guess.
        - Verify that the plan follows the expected structure when validator warnings or the outline suggest drift.
        - Additional flags may use these categories: correctness, security, completeness, performance, maintainability, other.
        - Focus on concrete issues, not structural formatting.

231	export function parseWorkerBackend(value?: string | null): WorkerBackend {
232	  const normalized = (value ?? "").trim().toLowerCase();
233	  if (normalized === "" || normalized === "wgp") return "wgp";
234	  if (normalized === "vibecomfy") return "vibecomfy";
235	  throw new Error(`Unsupported worker backend ${JSON.stringify(value)}; expected unset, 'wgp', or 'vibecomfy'`);
236	}
237	
238	export function deriveRouteKey(taskType: string, params: Record<string, unknown> | null = {}): string {
239	  const taskParams = params ?? {};
240	  const sourceTaskType = taskParams._source_task_type;
241	  if (typeof sourceTaskType === "string" && DIMENSIONAL_CHILD_TASK_TYPES.has(sourceTaskType)) {
242	    return dimensionalChildRouteKey(sourceTaskType, taskParams);
243	  }
244	  if (DIMENSIONAL_CHILD_TASK_TYPES.has(taskType)) {
245	    return dimensionalChildRouteKey(taskType, taskParams);
246	  }
247	  return directRouteKey(taskType);
248	}

250	export function routeSnapshotFields(input: RouteSnapshotInput): RouteSnapshotFields {
251	  const params = { ...(input.params ?? {}) };
252	  const selectedBackend = parseWorkerBackend(input.backend ?? "wgp");
253	  const selectorNamespace = input.selector_namespace ?? "production";
254	  const selectorVersion = input.selector_version ?? null;

256	  const selectorEntry = selectorEntryForRouteKey(routeKey);
257	  const supportState = selectorEntry?.support_state ?? "vibecomfy_unsupported";
258	  const templateId = selectorEntry?.template_id ?? null;
259	  const selectedProfile = String(input.profile ?? routeProfile(params));
260	  const routeRunId = input.run_id ?? null;
261	  const workerContractVersion = input.worker_contract_version ?? WORKER_ROUTE_CONTRACT_VERSION;
262	
263	  const snapshot: RouteSelectionSnapshot = {
264	    selector_namespace: selectorNamespace,
265	    route_key: routeKey,
266	    selected_backend: selectedBackend,
267	    selector_version: selectorVersion,
268	    support_state: supportState,
269	    template_id: templateId,
270	    selected_profile: selectedProfile,
271	    route_run_id: routeRunId,
272	    worker_contract_version: workerContractVersion,
273	  };
274	  if (input.task_id) snapshot.task_id = input.task_id;
275	  if (input.parent_route_key) snapshot.[REDACTED];
276	
277	  return {
278	    selector_namespace: selectorNamespace,
279	    route_key: routeKey,

291	export function selectorEntryForRouteKey(routeKey: string): RouteSelectorEntry | null {
292	  const selectorEntry = SPRINT_2_SELECTOR_MAP[routeKey];
293	  if (selectorEntry) return selectorEntry;
294	  const section3aEntry = SECTION3A_ROUTE_SUPPORT_MAP[routeKey];
295	  if (section3aEntry) return section3aEntry;
296	  if (
297	    routeKey.startsWith("travel_segment__") ||
298	    routeKey.startsWith("individual_travel_segment__") ||
299	    routeKey.startsWith("join_clips_segment__")
300	  ) {
301	    return { route_key: routeKey, support_state: "vibecomfy_unsupported", template_id: null };
302	  }
303	  return null;
304	}

306	export function classifyVibeComfyBlockerForEntry(
307	  routeKey: string,
308	  selectorEntry: RouteSelectorEntry | null,
309	): VibeComfyRouteBlockerReason | null {
310	  if (!selectorEntry) return "unknown";
311	  if (selectorEntry.route_key !== routeKey) return "malformed";
312	  if (selectorEntry.support_state === "wgp_only") return "wgp_only";
313	  if (selectorEntry.support_state === "vibecomfy_unsupported") return "unsupported";
314	  if (selectorEntry.vibecomfy_status === "fallback") return "fallback";
315	  if (selectorEntry.vibecomfy_status === "untested") return "untested";
316	  if (!selectorEntry.template_id) return "missing_template";
317	  return null;
318	}

353	export function selectedRouteRequirementFromContract(candidate: unknown): SelectedRouteRequirement {
354	  if (!isRecord(candidate)) {
355	    return malformedRouteRequirement();
356	  }
357	
358	  const snapshot = isRecord(candidate.route_selection_snapshot)
359	    ? candidate.route_selection_snapshot
360	    : candidate;
361	  const routeKey = typeof snapshot.route_key === "string" ? snapshot.route_key : null;
362	  const supportState = snapshot.support_state;
363	  const templateId = snapshot.template_id;
364	
365	  if (
366	    !routeKey ||
367	    (supportState !== "wgp_only" &&
368	      supportState !== "vibecomfy_supported" &&
369	      supportState !== "vibecomfy_unsupported") ||
370	    (templateId !== null && typeof templateId !== "string")
371	  ) {
372	    return malformedRouteRequirement();
373	  }
374	
375	  const selectorEntry: RouteSelectorEntry = {
376	    route_key: routeKey,
377	    support_state: supportState,
378	    template_id: templateId,
379	  };
380	
381	  return {
382	    task_type: routeKey,
383	    route_key: routeKey,
384	    support_state: supportState,
385	    template_id: templateId,
386	    role: "child",
387	    vibecomfy_blocker: classifyVibeComfyBlockerForEntry(routeKey, selectorEntry),
388	  };
389	}

417	    route_key: "malformed",

425	function directRouteKey(taskType: string): string {
426	  const slugged = slug(taskType);
427	  return DIRECT_ROUTE_ALIASES[slugged] ?? taskType;
428	}
429	
430	function dimensionalChildRouteKey(taskType: string, params: Record<string, unknown>): string {
431	  return [
432	    slug(taskType),
433	    `model-${slug(routeModelFamily(params))}`,
434	    `guidance-${slug(routeGuidanceKey(taskType, params))}`,
435	    `continuity-${slug(routeContinuityCase(taskType, params))}`,
436	    `profile-${slug(routeProfile(params))}`,
437	  ].join("__");
438	}
439	
440	function slug(value: unknown): string {
441	  const text = String(value || "none").trim().toLowerCase().replace(/\+/g, "_plus_");
442	  return text.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, "_").replace(/_+/g, "_").replace(/^_+|_+$/g, "") || "none";
443	}
444	
445	function routeModelFamily(params: Record<string, unknown>): string {
446	  const explicitFamily = params.model_family;
447	  if (explicitFamily) return String(explicitFamily);
448	
449	  const normalized = slug(params.model_name ?? params.model);
450	  if (!normalized || normalized === "none") return "unknown";
451	  if (normalized.includes("wan_2_2") || normalized.includes("wan22")) {
452	    return normalized.includes("vace") ? "wan22_vace" : "wan22_i2v";
453	  }
454	  if (normalized.includes("ltx2")) {
455	    return normalized.includes("distilled") ? "ltx2_distilled" : "ltx2";
456	  }
457	  if (normalized.includes("qwen")) return "qwen";
458	  if (normalized.includes("z_image")) return "z_image";
459	  return normalized;
460	}
461	
462	function routeGuidanceKind(taskType: string, params: Record<string, unknown>): string {
463	  const explicitKind = params.guidance_kind ?? params.travel_guidance_kind;
464	  if (explicitKind) return String(explicitKind);
465	
466	  const travelGuidance = params.travel_guidance;
467	  if (travelGuidance && typeof travelGuidance === "object" && "kind" in travelGuidance) {
468	    const kind = (travelGuidance as { kind?: unknown }).kind;
469	    if (kind) return String(kind);
470	  }
471	  if (params.use_uni3c || params.uni3c_guide_video) return "uni3c";
472	  if (params.svi2pro) return "vace";
473	  if (params.video_guide || params.video_mask) return "vace";
474	  if (taskType === "join_clips_segment" && routeModelFamily(params) === "wan22_vace") return "vace";
475	  return "none";
476	}
477	
478	function routeGuidanceMode(params: Record<string, unknown>): string {
479	  const explicitMode = params.guidance_mode ?? params.travel_guidance_mode;
480	  if (explicitMode) return String(explicitMode);
481	
482	  const travelGuidance = params.travel_guidance;
483	  if (travelGuidance && typeof travelGuidance === "object" && "mode" in travelGuidance) {
484	    const mode = (travelGuidance as { mode?: unknown }).mode;
485	    if (mode) return String(mode);
486	  }
487	  return "none";
488	}
489	
490	function routeGuidanceKey(taskType: string, params: Record<string, unknown>): string {
491	  const kind = routeGuidanceKind(taskType, params);
492	  const mode = routeGuidanceMode(params);
493	  if ((kind === "vace" || kind === "ltx_control") && mode && mode !== "none") {
494	    return `${kind}_${mode}`;
495	  }
496	  return kind;
497	}
498	
499	function routeContinuityCase(taskType: string, params: Record<string, unknown>): string {
500	  if (params.continuity_case) return String(params.continuity_case);
501	  if (taskType === "join_clips_segment") return "join_bridge";
502	  if (params.video_source) return "video_source";
503	  return "first_last";
504	}
505	
506	function routeProfile(params: Record<string, unknown>): string {
507	  return String(params.profile ?? params.wgp_profile ?? params.override_profile ?? "default");
508	}
509
```

> TOOL

tool_result Read
```
1	-- Reject inserts/updates that leave tasks.route_key NULL or blank.
2	--
3	-- The claim RPC (claim_next_task_service_role) joins each task to
4	-- route_backend_claim_decision(selector_namespace, route_key, worker_backend).
5	-- Tasks with a NULL or empty route_key are silently unclaimable. The
6	-- create-task edge function now mirrors params.route_contract.route_key onto
7	-- the top-level column via stampTaskRouteContract; this constraint catches
8	-- any future code path that bypasses that helper at write time.
9	--
10	-- We use NOT VALID so the constraint is enforced for new INSERTs/UPDATEs
11	-- without rejecting the ~45k legacy Complete/Failed rows that were written
12	-- before route_key existed. Backfilling those is out of scope (they're not
13	-- claimable anyway because they're not Queued).
14	
15	BEGIN;
16	
17	ALTER TABLE public.tasks
18	  ADD CONSTRAINT tasks_route_key_required
19	  CHECK (route_key IS NOT NULL AND length(trim(route_key)) > 0)
20	  NOT VALID;
21	
22	COMMENT ON CONSTRAINT tasks_route_key_required ON public.tasks IS
23	  'Tasks must carry a non-empty route_key (mirrored from params.route_contract.route_key). Enforced for new inserts/updates; legacy pre-route-contract rows are not validated.';
24	
25	COMMIT;
26
```

tool_result Read
```
1	-- Add route-contract filtering to service-role task claiming.
2	--
3	-- Existing callers using the 7-argument pool-aware claim function continue to
4	-- work through PostgreSQL defaults. New canary workers pass backend/profile/
5	-- selector/contract fields so stale workers cannot claim tasks selected for a
6	-- different route contract.
7	
8	BEGIN;
9	
10	DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS public.claim_next_task_service_role(TEXT, BOOLEAN, TEXT, BOOLEAN, INT, TEXT, TEXT[]);
11	
12	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION claim_next_task_service_role(
13	  p_worker_id TEXT,
14	  p_include_active BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
15	  p_run_type TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
16	  p_same_model_only BOOLEAN DEFAULT FALSE,
17	  p_max_task_wait_minutes INT DEFAULT 5,
18	  p_worker_pool TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
19	  p_task_types TEXT[] DEFAULT NULL,
20	  p_worker_backend TEXT DEFAULT 'wgp',
21	  p_worker_profile TEXT DEFAULT 'default',
22	  p_selector_namespace TEXT DEFAULT 'production',
23	  p_selector_version TEXT DEFAULT NULL,
24	  p_worker_contract_version INT DEFAULT 1
25	)
26	RETURNS TABLE(
27	  task_id UUID,
28	  params JSONB,
29	  task_type TEXT,
30	  project_id UUID,
31	  user_id UUID,
32	  selector_namespace TEXT,
33	  route_key TEXT,
34	  selected_backend TEXT,
35	  selector_version TEXT,
36	  selected_profile TEXT,
37	  selected_template_id TEXT,
38	  route_run_id TEXT,
39	  worker_contract_version INT,
40	  claim_decision_reason TEXT
41	)
42	LANGUAGE plpgsql
43	SECURITY DEFINER
44	AS $$
45	DECLARE
46	  v_task_id UUID;
47	  v_params JSONB;
48	  v_task_type TEXT;
49	  v_project_id UUID;
50	  v_user_id UUID;
51	  v_selector_namespace TEXT;
52	  v_route_key TEXT;
53	  v_selected_backend TEXT;
54	  v_selector_version TEXT;
55	  v_selected_profile TEXT;
56	  v_selected_template_id TEXT;
57	  v_route_run_id TEXT;
58	  v_worker_contract_version INT;
59	  v_status_filter task_status[];
60	  v_worker_model TEXT;
61	  v_has_starving_task BOOLEAN := FALSE;
62	  v_no_matching_tasks BOOLEAN := FALSE;
63	  v_effective_max_task_wait_minutes INT := COALESCE(p_max_task_wait_minutes, 5);
64	  v_is_banodoco_pool BOOLEAN := (p_worker_pool = 'banodoco');
65	  v_has_task_types_filter BOOLEAN := (p_task_types IS NOT NULL AND array_length(p_task_types, 1) > 0);
66	  v_worker_backend TEXT := COALESCE(NULLIF(p_worker_backend, ''), 'wgp');
67	  v_worker_profile TEXT := COALESCE(NULLIF(p_worker_profile, ''), 'default');
68	  v_selector_namespace_filter TEXT := COALESCE(NULLIF(p_selector_namespace, ''), 'production');
69	  v_selector_version_filter TEXT := COALESCE(p_selector_version, '');
70	  v_contract_version_filter INT := COALESCE(p_worker_contract_version, 1);
71	BEGIN
72	  IF p_include_active THEN
73	    v_status_filter := ARRAY['Queued'::task_status, 'In Progress'::task_status];
74	  ELSE
75	    v_status_filter := ARRAY['Queued'::task_status];
76	  END IF;
77	
78	  SELECT current_model INTO v_worker_model
79	  FROM workers
80	  WHERE id = p_worker_id AND status = 'active';
81	
82	  CREATE TEMP TABLE _eligible_users ON COMMIT DROP AS
83	    SELECT
84	      u.id as user_id,
85	      u.credits,
86	      COALESCE((u.settings->'ui'->'generationMethods'->>'inCloud')::boolean, true) as allows_cloud,
87	      COUNT(in_progress_tasks.id) as in_progress_count
88	    FROM users u
89	    LEFT JOIN projects p ON p.user_id = u.id
90	    LEFT JOIN tasks in_progress_tasks ON in_progress_tasks.project_id = p.id
91	      AND in_progress_tasks.status = 'In Progress'::task_status
92	      AND COALESCE(in_progress_tasks.task_type, '') NOT ILIKE '%orchestrator%'
93	    WHERE u.credits > 0
94	      AND COALESCE((u.settings->'ui'->'generationMethods'->>'inCloud')::boolean, true) = true
95	    GROUP BY u.id, u.credits, u.settings
96	    HAVING COUNT(in_progress_tasks.id) < 5;
97	
98	  IF p_same_model_only AND v_worker_model IS NOT NULL THEN
99	    SELECT
100	      NOT bool_or(get_task_model(t.params) = v_worker_model),
101	      bool_or(t.created_at < NOW() - (v_effective_max_task_wait_minutes || ' minutes')::interval)
102	    INTO v_no_matching_tasks, v_has_starving_task
103	    FROM tasks t
104	    JOIN projects p ON t.project_id = p.id
105	    CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT t.params->'route_contract' AS rc) route
106	    WHERE t.status = 'Queued'::task_status
107	      AND all_dependencies_complete(t.dependant_on)
108	      AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM _eligible_users eu WHERE eu.user_id = p.user_id)
109	      AND (p_run_type IS NULL OR get_task_run_type(t.task_type) = p_run_type)
110	      AND (
111	        (v_is_banodoco_pool AND t.task_type LIKE 'banodoco\_%' ESCAPE '\')
112	        OR (NOT v_is_banodoco_pool AND t.task_type NOT LIKE 'banodoco\_%' ESCAPE '\')
113	      )
114	      AND (NOT v_has_task_types_filter OR t.task_type = ANY(p_task_types))
115	      AND (
116	        route.rc IS NULL
117	        OR (
118	          route.rc->>'selected_backend' IN ('wgp', 'vibecomfy')
119	          AND COALESCE(route.rc->>'selector_namespace', '') <> ''
120	          AND COALESCE(route.rc->>'selected_profile', '') <> ''
121	          AND COALESCE(route.rc->>'worker_contract_version', '') ~ '^[0-9]+$'
122	        )
123	      )
124	      AND COALESCE(route.rc->>'selected_backend', 'wgp') = v_worker_backend
125	      AND COALESCE(route.rc->>'selector_namespace', 'production') = v_selector_namespace_filter
126	      AND COALESCE(route.rc->>'selector_version', '') = v_selector_version_filter
127	      AND (
128	        CASE
129	          WHEN COALESCE(route.rc->>'worker_contract_version', '') ~ '^[0-9]+$'
130	            THEN (route.rc->>'worker_contract_version')::INT
131	          ELSE 1
132	        END
133	      ) = v_contract_version_filter
134	      AND (
135	        COALESCE(route.rc->>'selected_profile', 'default') = v_worker_profile
136	        OR (v_worker_profile = '1' AND COALESCE(route.rc->>'selected_profile', 'default') = 'default')
137	        OR (v_worker_profile = 'default' AND COALESCE(route.rc->>'selected_profile', 'default') = '1')
138	      );
139	
140	    v_no_matching_tasks := COALESCE(v_no_matching_tasks, TRUE);
141	    v_has_starving_task := COALESCE(v_has_starving_task, FALSE);
142	  END IF;
143	
144	  WITH ready_tasks AS (
145	    SELECT
146	      t.id,
147	      t.params,
148	      t.task_type,
149	      t.project_id,
150	      t.created_at,
151	      p.user_id,
152	      COALESCE(route.rc->>'selector_namespace', 'production') AS route_selector_namespace,
153	      COALESCE(route.rc->>'route_key', t.task_type) AS route_key,
154	      COALESCE(route.rc->>'selected_backend', 'wgp') AS route_selected_backend,
155	      NULLIF(route.rc->>'selector_version', '') AS route_selector_version,
156	      COALESCE(route.rc->>'selected_profile', 'default') AS route_selected_profile,
157	      NULLIF(route.rc->>'selected_template_id', '') AS route_selected_template_id,
158	      NULLIF(route.rc->>'route_run_id', '') AS route_run_id,
159	      (
160	        CASE
161	          WHEN COALESCE(route.rc->>'worker_contract_version', '') ~ '^[0-9]+$'
162	            THEN (route.rc->>'worker_contract_version')::INT
163	          ELSE 1
164	        END
165	      ) AS route_worker_contract_version,
166	      ROW_NUMBER() OVER (
167	        ORDER BY
168	          CASE
169	            WHEN v_has_starving_task OR v_no_matching_tasks THEN NULL
170	            WHEN v_worker_model IS NOT NULL AND get_task_model(t.params) = v_worker_model THEN 0
171	            ELSE 1
172	          END NULLS LAST,
173	          t.created_at ASC
174	      ) as rn
175	    FROM tasks t
176	    JOIN projects p ON t.project_id = p.id
177	    CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT t.params->'route_contract' AS rc) route
178	    WHERE t.status = 'Queued'::task_status
179	      AND all_dependencies_complete(t.dependant_on)
180	      AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM _eligible_users eu WHERE eu.user_id = p.user_id)
181	      AND (p_run_type IS NULL OR get_task_run_type(t.task_type) = p_run_type)
182	      AND (
183	        (v_is_banodoco_pool AND t.task_type LIKE 'banodoco\_%' ESCAPE '\')
184	        OR (NOT v_is_banodoco_pool AND t.task_type NOT LIKE 'banodoco\_%' ESCAPE '\')
185	      )
186	      AND (NOT v_has_task_types_filter OR t.task_type = ANY(p_task_types))
187	      AND (
188	        v_has_starving_task OR v_no_matching_tasks OR NOT p_same_model_only
189	        OR v_worker_model IS NULL OR get_task_model(t.params) = v_worker_model
190	      )
191	      AND (
192	        route.rc IS NULL
193	        OR (
194	          route.rc->>'selected_backend' IN ('wgp', 'vibecomfy')
195	          AND COALESCE(route.rc->>'selector_namespace', '') <> ''
196	          AND COALESCE(route.rc->>'selected_profile', '') <> ''
197	          AND COALESCE(route.rc->>'worker_contract_version', '') ~ '^[0-9]+$'
198	        )
199	      )
200	      AND COALESCE(route.rc->>'selected_backend', 'wgp') = v_worker_backend
201	      AND COALESCE(route.rc->>'selector_namespace', 'production') = v_selector_namespace_filter
202	      AND COALESCE(route.rc->>'selector_version', '') = v_selector_version_filter
203	      AND (
204	        CASE
205	          WHEN COALESCE(route.rc->>'worker_contract_version', '') ~ '^[0-9]+$'
206	            THEN (route.rc->>'worker_contract_version')::INT
207	          ELSE 1
208	        END
209	      ) = v_contract_version_filter
210	      AND (
211	        COALESCE(route.rc->>'selected_profile', 'default') = v_worker_profile
212	        OR (v_worker_profile = '1' AND COALESCE(route.rc->>'selected_profile', 'default') = 'default')
213	        OR (v_worker_profile = 'default' AND COALESCE(route.rc->>'selected_profile', 'default') = '1')
214	      )
215	  )
216	  UPDATE tasks
217	  SET
218	    status = CASE WHEN status = 'Queued'::task_status THEN 'In Progress'::task_status ELSE status END,
219	    worker_id = CASE WHEN status = 'Queued'::task_status THEN p_worker_id ELSE worker_id END,
220	    updated_at = CASE WHEN status = 'Queued'::task_status THEN NOW() ELSE updated_at END,
221	    generation_started_at = CASE WHEN status = 'Queued'::task_status THEN NOW() ELSE generation_started_at END
222	  FROM ready_tasks rt
223	  WHERE tasks.id = rt.id
224	    AND rt.rn = 1
225	    AND (NOT p_include_active OR tasks.status = 'Queued'::task_status)
226	  RETURNING
227	    tasks.id,
228	    tasks.params,
229	    tasks.task_type,
230	    tasks.project_id,
231	    rt.user_id,
232	    rt.route_selector_namespace,
233	    rt.route_key,
234	    rt.route_selected_backend,
235	    rt.route_selector_version,
236	    rt.route_selected_profile,
237	    rt.route_selected_template_id,
238	    rt.route_run_id,
239	    rt.route_worker_contract_version
240	  INTO
241	    v_task_id,
242	    v_params,
243	    v_task_type,
244	    v_project_id,
245	    v_user_id,
246	    v_selector_namespace,
247	    v_route_key,
248	    v_selected_backend,
249	    v_selector_version,
250	    v_selected_profile,
251	    v_selected_template_id,
252	    v_route_run_id,
253	    v_worker_contract_version;
254	
255	  IF v_task_id IS NOT NULL THEN
256	    task_id := v_task_id;
257	    params := v_params;
258	    task_type := v_task_type;
259	    project_id := v_project_id;
260	    user_id := v_user_id;
261	    selector_namespace := v_selector_namespace;
262	    route_key := v_route_key;
263	    selected_backend := v_selected_backend;
264	    selector_version := v_selector_version;
265	    selected_profile := v_selected_profile;
266	    selected_template_id := v_selected_template_id;
267	    route_run_id := v_route_run_id;
268	    worker_contract_version := v_worker_contract_version;
269	    claim_decision_reason := 'eligible';
270	    RETURN NEXT;
271	  END IF;
272	
273	  RETURN;
274	END;
275	$$;
276	
277	COMMENT ON FUNCTION claim_next_task_service_role(TEXT, BOOLEAN, TEXT, BOOLEAN, INT, TEXT, TEXT[], TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, TEXT, INT) IS
278	'Claims next eligible task for service role with worker pool and selected-route contract filtering. New canary workers pass backend/profile/selector/contract fields so stale workers receive no task before assignment/status transition.';
279	
280	COMMIT;
281
```

```
179	CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION public.route_backend_claim_decision(
180	  p_selector_namespace text,
181	  p_route_key text,
182	  p_worker_backend text,
183	  p_now timestamptz DEFAULT now()
184	)
185	RETURNS TABLE(
186	  selector_namespace text,
187	  route_key text,
188	  worker_backend text,
189	  selected_backend text,
190	  selector_version bigint,
191	  selector_present boolean,
192	  selector_enabled boolean,
193	  selector_expired boolean,
194	  capability_present boolean,
195	  capability_version bigint,
196	  capability_supports_route boolean,
197	  capability_supports_missing_selector boolean,
198	  eligible boolean,
199	  decision_reason text,
200	  selector_snapshot jsonb,
201	  capability_snapshot jsonb
202	)
203	LANGUAGE sql
204	STABLE
205	SECURITY DEFINER
206	SET search_path = public
207	AS $$
208	  WITH normalized AS (
209	    SELECT
210	      COALESCE(NULLIF(p_selector_namespace, ''), 'production') AS selector_namespace,
211	      p_route_key AS route_key,
212	      p_worker_backend AS worker_backend,
213	      p_now AS decision_at
214	  ),
215	  selector_row AS (
216	    SELECT s.*
217	    FROM public.route_backend_selectors s
218	    JOIN normalized n
219	      ON s.selector_namespace = n.selector_namespace
220	     AND s.route_key = n.route_key
221	    LIMIT 1
222	  ),
223	  capability_row AS (
224	    SELECT c.*
225	    FROM public.route_backend_capabilities c
226	    JOIN normalized n
227	      ON c.backend = n.worker_backend
228	     AND c.route_key = n.route_key
229	    LIMIT 1
230	  )
231	  SELECT
232	    n.selector_namespace,
233	    n.route_key,
234	    n.worker_backend,
235	    s.selected_backend,
236	    s.selector_version,
237	    (s.id IS NOT NULL) AS selector_present,
238	    COALESCE(s.enabled, false) AS selector_enabled,
239	    COALESCE(s.expires_at <= n.decision_at, false) AS selector_expired,
240	    (c.id IS NOT NULL) AS capability_present,
241	    c.capability_version,
242	    COALESCE(c.supports_route AND c.enabled AND (c.expires_at IS NULL OR c.expires_at > n.decision_at), false)
243	      AS capability_supports_route,
244	    COALESCE(c.supports_missing_selector AND c.enabled AND (c.expires_at IS NULL OR c.expires_at > n.decision_at), false)
245	      AS capability_supports_missing_selector,
246	    CASE
247	      WHEN n.worker_backend NOT IN ('wgp', 'vibecomfy') THEN false
248	      WHEN n.route_key IS NULL OR n.route_key = '' OR n.route_key ~ '\s' THEN false
249	      WHEN c.id IS NULL THEN false
250	      WHEN s.id IS NULL THEN
251	        n.worker_backend = 'wgp'
252	        AND COALESCE(c.supports_missing_selector AND c.enabled AND (c.expires_at IS NULL OR c.expires_at > n.decision_at), false)
253	      ELSE
254	        s.enabled
255	        AND (s.expires_at IS NULL OR s.expires_at > n.decision_at)
256	        AND s.selected_backend = n.worker_backend
257	        AND COALESCE(c.supports_route AND c.enabled AND (c.expires_at IS NULL OR c.expires_at > n.decision_at), false)
258	    END AS eligible,
259	    CASE
260	      WHEN n.worker_backend NOT IN ('wgp', 'vibecomfy') THEN 'malformed_worker_backend'
261	      WHEN n.route_key IS NULL OR n.route_key = '' OR n.route_key ~ '\s' THEN 'malformed_route_key'
262	      WHEN c.id IS NULL THEN 'missing_capability'
263	      WHEN s.id IS NULL AND n.worker_backend = 'vibecomfy' THEN 'missing_selector_vibecomfy_no_claim'
264	      WHEN s.id IS NULL AND NOT COALESCE(c.supports_missing_selector AND c.enabled AND (c.expires_at IS NULL OR c.expires_at > n.decision_at), false) THEN 'missing_selector_capability_unsupported'
265	      WHEN s.id IS NULL THEN 'missing_selector_wgp_capability_supported'
266	      WHEN NOT s.enabled THEN 'selector_disabled'
267	      WHEN s.expires_at IS NOT NULL AND s.expires_at <= n.decision_at THEN 'selector_expired'
268	      WHEN s.selected_backend <> n.worker_backend THEN 'backend_mismatch'
269	      WHEN NOT COALESCE(c.enabled, false) THEN 'capability_disabled'
270	      WHEN c.expires_at IS NOT NULL AND c.expires_at <= n.decision_at THEN 'capability_expired'
271	      WHEN NOT COALESCE(c.supports_route, false) THEN 'capability_unsupported'
272	      ELSE 'eligible'
273	    END AS decision_reason,
274	    CASE
275	      WHEN s.id IS NULL THEN NULL
276	      ELSE jsonb_build_object(
277	        'selector_namespace', s.selector_namespace,
278	        'route_key', s.route_key,
279	        'selected_backend', s.selected_backend,
280	        'selector_version', s.selector_version,
281	        'enabled', s.enabled,
282	        'expires_at', s.expires_at,
283	        'min_worker_version', s.min_worker_version
284	      )
285	    END AS selector_snapshot,
286	    CASE
287	      WHEN c.id IS NULL THEN NULL
288	      ELSE jsonb_build_object(
289	        'backend', c.backend,
290	        'route_key', c.route_key,
291	        'supports_route', c.supports_route,
292	        'supports_missing_selector', c.supports_missing_selector,
293	        'capability_version', c.capability_version,
294	        'enabled', c.enabled,
295	        'expires_at', c.expires_at,
296	        'min_worker_version', c.min_worker_version
297	      )
298	    END AS capability_snapshot
299	  FROM normalized n
300	  LEFT JOIN selector_row s ON true
301	  LEFT JOIN capability_row c ON true;
302	$$;
303	
304	REVOKE EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.route_backend_claim_decision(text, text, text, timestamptz) FROM anon, authenticated;
305	GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION public.route_backend_claim_decision(text, text, text, timestamptz) TO service_role;
306

308	  backend,

260	        # (600s). Since we don't track idle-start time, there's no safe way to
261	        # implement this check. The ACTIVE_STALE check (heartbeat timeout) already
262	        # catches truly dead workers.
263	
264	    if is_route_stale and not has_active_task and not should_terminate:
265	        # Route-stale workers are intentionally drained through capacity handling:
266	        # active tasks are allowed to finish, idle/still-spawning workers are
267	        # terminated as normal scale actions instead of health failures.
268	        termination_reason = _route_stale_reason(worker_route_contract, current_route_contract)
269	        error_code = "STALE_ROUTE_CONTRACT"
270	
271	    elif lifecycle == WorkerLifecycle.ACTIVE_INITIALIZING:
272	        if queued_count > 0 and not has_ever_claimed:
273	            if effective_age_sec > config.startup_grace_period_sec:
274	                should_terminate = True
275	                termination_reason = f"Never initialized after startup grace period ({effective_age_sec:.0f}s)"
276	                error_code = "STARTUP_NEVER_READY"
277	
278	    elif lifecycle in (WorkerLifecycle.SPAWNING_POD_PENDING,
279	                       WorkerLifecycle.SPAWNING_SCRIPT_PENDING):
280	        if worker_age_sec > config.spawning_timeout_sec:
281	            should_terminate = True
282	            termination_reason = f"Spawning timeout ({worker_age_sec:.0f}s)"
283	            error_code = "SPAWNING_TIMEOUT"
284	
285	    elif lifecycle == WorkerLifecycle.SPAWNING_SCRIPT_RUNNING:
286	        # Script is running (installing deps, building, etc.) — use a longer

        Engineering brief produced from the codebase and task details:

        ### Task Summary
        Ship a 4-layer + cleanup runtime guardrail set across reigh-app (Supabase/Postgres + edge functions), reigh-worker (Python on RunPod), and reigh-worker-orchestrator (Python on Railway) so that cross-boundary contract drift becomes structurally loud (and in two spots impossible). Layers: (0) data triage + disambiguate `params.model_family`, (1) DB BEFORE INSERT/UPDATE trigger on `tasks` calling `route_backend_claim_decision`, (2) move route-key derivation into Postgres functions `derive_route_key` / `build_route_contract` and shrink TS+Python derivation to RPC wrappers, (3) convert three "log-only" detectors into kill/exit gates, (4) add a 60-second sentinel cron that pages on persistent unclaimable-work / stuck-init states. Explicitly NOT building a codegen contracts package.

        ### Key Evidence
        - stampTaskRouteContract ALREADY centralizes route_contract building today — it is called from create-task/index.ts before insert. The brief's claim 'Zero resolvers build a route_contract' is technically correct but misleading: building is intentionally centralized, not absent. Layer 2's job is to move *derivation* (routeSnapshotFields/routeModelFamily logic) into the DB, not to add centralization where none exists. (source: reigh-app/supabase/functions/create-task/routeContract.ts:150-200; relevance: high)
- A NOT NULL CHECK constraint on tasks.route_key already shipped TODAY (migration timestamp 20260513111812). The new Layer 1 trigger must compose with this: it adds the route_contract object + capability-eligibility checks, not the bare NOT NULL guard. (source: reigh-app/supabase/migrations/20260513111812_require_route_key.sql; relevance: high)
- The literal string 'Timed out waiting for worker preflight after 900s' does NOT exist anywhere in reigh-worker or reigh-worker-orchestrator on the working tree. Done-criterion #4 cannot be satisfied by grepping for that string verbatim. The brief is either citing a deleted line, a string from a different repo (orchestrator's `worker_startup.template.sh`?), or paraphrasing. Must hunt with broader patterns ('preflight', 'Timed out', 'PREFLIGHT') and confirm with user before editing. (source: Grep across both worker repos — zero matches; relevance: high)
- No existing cron / pg_cron / scheduled-function infrastructure exists in reigh-app or reigh-worker-orchestrator. Layer 4 sentinel is greenfield infra. Railway.json is minimal (build only, no cron entrypoint). Smallest-footprint choice: a Supabase Edge Function on a pg_cron schedule (single repo, no new Railway service), OR a tiny Python script as a Railway cron service. (source: reigh-worker-orchestrator/railway.json + grep for pg_cron/cron.schedule in reigh-app/supabase; relevance: high)
- `route_backend_capabilities` table and `route_backend_claim_decision` RPC are referenced in code comments and in the 20260513 migration but their CREATE statements are NOT in the current migrations/ tree (presumably in an older migration replaced by an `_applied_` prefixed file). Must query live DB for their schemas before writing the trigger that calls the RPC. Cleanup also expects to insert missing capability rows for `qwen_image_style` and `animate_character` — confirm the table's column shape first. (source: Grep results: only refs in comments at routeContract.ts:180, resolvers/types.ts:36, 20260513111812 migration; relevance: high)
- Worker writes `params['model_family'] = _derive_model_family(model_name)` (a DERIVED value, not bare 'wan' literal as brief implies). The cross-namespace collision is still the bug: `_derive_model_family` returns the worker-internal {ltx,wan} enum, then both TS routeModelFamily and Python _route_model_family honor that as override before normalizing. The Layer-0 rename to `model_family_class` must update this assignment AND the call to _derive_model_family's namespace, not just the two enum-check sites. (source: reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/travel/orchestrator.py:436-437; relevance: high)
- model_family enum-check callsites in worker are at task_registry.py:113-117, 300-302, 1157-1163 AND additional sites in matrix.py (197, 205, 436, 463) and test_primitives.py. Renaming to model_family_class touches at least 8 callsites across registry + matrix fixtures + tests, not the 3 the brief enumerates. (source: reigh-worker/source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py + grep results from Explore subagent; relevance: high)
- create-task has 27 resolver files in `resolvers/` plus a `__tests__` directory. Existing tests: `routeContract.test.ts`, `claim-next-task/index.test.ts`. The test for stampTaskRouteContract is the regression net for any change to route_contract shape that Layer 2 introduces — read it before editing. (source: reigh-app/supabase/functions/create-task/resolvers/ and routeContract.test.ts; relevance: medium)
- Branch posture per the brief: each repo currently has unrelated WIP on its sprint branch. The plan must explicitly branch fresh from origin/main of each repo (fix/contract-enforcement-*) and not push to sprint-07/sprint-09 branches because Railway auto-deploys from sprint-07 in the orchestrator and there is concurrent uncommitted work in reigh-app on sprint-09 (routeContract.ts WIP plus ~20 unrelated UI edits). (source: Task brief 'Concurrent-work hazard' + gitStatus showing docs/vibecomfy-post-chain-qwen-proof branch; relevance: high)

After Layer 1 ships, this insert path must continue to produce rows that pass the trigger — explicitly listed in the Done Criteria as 'no regression in live-test path'. - Worker preflight script search produced `reigh-worker/scripts/live_test/preflight.py` (a safety gate for live-test, not pod launcher). The pod launcher logic likely lives in `reigh-worker-orchestrator` worker_startup.template.sh or in a script invoked by it. Need to locate it precisely before claiming the launcher-exit conversion is done. (source: Explore subagent search result; relevance: medium)
- Orchestrator detector at worker_state.py:271-276 currently reads: `if queued_count > 0 and not has_ever_claimed and effective_age_sec > config.startup_grace_period_sec: terminate('STARTUP_NEVER_READY')`. Dropping queued_count gate is a one-line change but config.startup_grace_period_sec must be a sensible value for the empty-queue case (e.g., not so short it flaps when queue genuinely empty during normal pod warmup). The trigger uses BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE so existing rows are safe UNTIL someone updates them, but a Layer-0 sweep is still required for the 'new freshly-created travel_orchestrator end-to-end' done-criterion to pass cleanly. Layer 1's new trigger should follow the same pattern (BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE WHEN NEW.status = 'Queued') to avoid scanning legacy rows, and must exempt `task_type LIKE '%_orchestrator'` as the brief specifies. (source: reigh-app/supabase/migrations/20260513111812_require_route_key.sql lines 10-13; relevance: high)

        ### Test Expectations
        - [pass_to_pass] reigh-app/supabase/functions/create-task/routeContract.test.ts (existing): stampTaskRouteContract output shape, ROUTE_CONTRACT_PARAM_KEY normalization, route_key + selector mirroring onto top-level columns.
